# FORBIDDEN DOCTRINE FINDINGS

## Status

Mapping only. No language, ARB, generated file, or runtime changes were made.

## Doctrine Summary

| Doctrine | Findings |
|---|---:|
| Control Doctrine | 847 |
| Booking Doctrine | 667 |
| Ownership Doctrine | 409 |
| Payment Doctrine | 285 |
| Assignment Doctrine | 158 |
| Diagnostic Doctrine | 131 |
| Approval Doctrine | 98 |
| Admin Doctrine | 58 |

## Doctrine Rule

A finding is blocked when it restores forbidden authority: booking ownership, admin authority, assignment, approval, ownership of cases or queues, control-room doctrine, payment/payout/accounting lifecycle, or diagnostic/deficit framing. Transitional words become BLOCK when they carry one of those authority models.
